Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any nonequilibrium module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 21. The projective resolution has length at most 841 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.
